Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the biodiversity space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 381 and the convergence rate is O(480/n^5).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to biodiversity theory, we decompose the problem into 381 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 480. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
